Audited the printed graveyard interactions on Rest in Peace (#491), Leyline of the Void (#491), Tormod's Crypt (#472), Endurance, Cling to Dust, Surgical Extraction, Nihil Spellbomb, Relic of Progenitus, Scavenging Ooze — all wired end-to-end (ETB exile / static graveyard rewrite / target-player graveyard sweep / discard-cost graveyard exile / phyrexian global name exile / escape alt-cost). **Soul-Guide Lantern not yet shipped** (no `SoulGuideLanternFactory.cs` on the branch — the audit prompt listed it as "verify shipped"; flagged as a follow-up). Shipped two new factories: `BojukaBogFactory` ships the Worldwake / MH2 Land with the unconditional `EntersTappedReplacement` (CR 614.1c) + an ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` whose 1..1 "target player" `TargetRequest` resolution snapshots the chosen player's graveyard and moves every card to that player's `ZoneType.Exile` (CR 608.2b — empty graveyard is a clean no-op; v1 deterministic controller fallback mirrors Tormod's Crypt / Nihil Spellbomb), plus `{T}: Add {B}` `ManaAbility` declared inline (`OracleManaBinder` only auto-binds the subtype-derived colour for Basic lands). `FaerieMacabreFactory` ships the Morningtide Creature — Faerie Rogue {1}{B}{B} 2/2 with Flash + Flying `KeywordAbility` markers + a Discard-from-hand activated ability (CR 117) whose sole cost is `DiscardSelfCost(card)` — same primitive every Channel land uses (CR 702.74a-style zone gate via `DiscardSelfCost.CanPay`, so `ActionValidator` rejects activation outside the controller's hand without extra wiring). Single 0..2 "target card in a graveyard" `TargetRequest` (CR 115.1b — "up to two" means `MinTargets=0`); resolution iterates picks (capped at 2), per-target legality recheck on `Zone == Graveyard` + non-null Owner (CR 608.2b illegal pick skipped per-target, other legal picks still resolve), moves each to its owner's `ZoneType.Exile`. **Scavenging Ooze audit**: factory's existing oracle interpretation is the printed-original "Exile target creature card from a graveyard. If you do, put a +1/+1 counter and gain 1 life" — wired end-to-end (cross-graveyard `allPlayersResolver` scan, deterministic first-creature pick, `CountersService` was not yet routed at write-time so the +1/+1 placement uses `card.Counters.Add` directly — same posture as Endurance pre-`CountersService` retrofit); the more recent "exile target CARD, then check if it was a creature" oracle variant is a deferred re-wording (no behaviour change for the dominant card-target case). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/BojukaBogFactoryTests.cs` (8 tests — identity, dispatch, ETB-tapped wired vs shape-only, mana ability, target-player graveyard sweep, empty graveyard fizzle, controller fallback) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/FaerieMacabreFactoryTests.cs` (9 tests — identity, dispatch, Flash + Flying markers, `DiscardSelfCost` cost shape + `MinTargets=0` / `MaxTargets=2`, hand-zone gate via `DiscardSelfCost.CanPay`, two-target exile, no-target no-op, per-target illegal-on-resolution skip, single-target exile). All 5448+ Core tests + 341 Server tests + 149 Api tests + 135 Bot tests green. Coverage NamedFactory tier 301 → 303.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Bloodbraid Elf + Violent Outburst + Cascade audit. `BloodbraidElfFactory` ships the Alara Reborn / MH2 3/2 Creature — Elf Berserker {2}{R}{G} with Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — `CombatAbilities.HasHaste` consumer) and the printed Cascade triggered ability (CR 702.85) over `SpellCastEvent` filtered to this card with `sourceManaValue: 4`. Same wiring shape as `ShardlessAgentFactory` / `CrashingFootfallsFactory` / `LivingEndFactory`: caller-supplied `willCast` decision + `onCascadeResolved` callback for production hosts to drive the free cast through `SpellCastFlow` + `CastFromExileAlternativeCost(ManaCost.Zero)`. `ViolentOutburstFactory` ships the Alara Reborn Instant {1}{R}{G} with the same cascade trigger shape at `sourceManaValue: 3` plus a resolve-time `BuildSpellDefinition(caster)` that snapshots `controller.Zones.Battlefield.GetCards().OfType<Creature>()` and registers `PumpUntilEndOfTurnEffect(+1, 0)` (Layer 7c per CR 613.1c) + `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect("Haste")` (Layer 6 — CR 702.10) on each creature's `ActiveEffects`; both expire on cleanup (CR 514.2). **Cascade audit confirmed type-agnostic** — the existing `CascadeAction.Cascade` takes only `(Player controller, int sourceManaValue)` independent of card type, and the trigger condition keys on `ReferenceEquals(e.Spell.Card, card)` so the instant-speed cascade source (Violent Outburst) fires identically to the sorcery-speed source (Crashing Footfalls). `TriggeredAbility.ActiveZones = {Stack}` matches Crashing Footfalls / Living End / Shardless Agent. Both cards registered in `CascadeAltCostProbe.DefaultIsCascadeCard` so the bot's bidding heuristic / value layer recognises them as cascade cards. New `CardSubtype.Berserker` (creature subtype, CR 205.3m — Bloodbraid Elf). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/BloodbraidElfTests.cs` (6 tests — identity, dispatch, Haste keyword, cascade trigger fires with `sourceMV: 4`, cascade hits MV-3 boundary, cascade discovery) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/ViolentOutburstTests.cs` (10 tests — identity, dispatch, cascade trigger fires at instant-speed with `sourceMV: 3`, `ActiveZones` includes `Stack`, resolve pumps controller's creatures only and grants Haste, empty-battlefield no-op, no-`ActiveEffects` no-op, condition matches only this card's cast, cascade discovery).)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Player-hexproof primitive + Leyline of Sanctity full retrofit. New `Majik.Core/Rules/PlayerStaticAbilities.cs` — side-channel registry mirroring `CastingRestrictions`, keyed by (source-token, player) so multiple sources stack without trampling and a player has hexproof while any entry survives. `Player.HasHexproof` (new property on `Majik.Core/Players/Player.cs`) proxies `PlayerStaticAbilities.HasHexproof(this)` so any caller can ask. New `Majik.Core/Effects/PlayerHexproofEffect.cs` lifecycle mirrors `CastFromHandOnlyRestrictionEffect` / `SorcerySpeedRestrictionEffect` — `Permanent? source` + `IEventBus?` + `affectedPlayersResolver` + optional `activeWhile`, subscribes `CardMovedEvent` on Attach, syncs ETB/LTB, idempotent, supports emblem-style always-on usage. `ActionValidator.ValidateCastSpell` + `ValidateActivateAbility` now consult an optional new `CastSpellAction.Targets` / `ActivateAbilityAction.Targets` collection: any `Player` target with `HasHexproof` that isn't the caster rejects the action with `RuleViolation 702.11` ("player-hexproof"); self-targeting passes (CR 113.5b — "controlled by opponents" only). `Targeting.TargetLegality.IsLegal` extended to also reject opponent-controlled `Player` candidates with player-hexproof at resolution-time recheck (CR 608.2b). `LeylineOfSanctityFactory` retrofit: new `Create(owner, IEventBus?)` overload wires a `PlayerHexproofEffect` against the live controller (re-resolved at sync time so control-change effects shift the grant); single-arg overload still constructs without the bus for dispatcher tests. Deferred docstring dropped — Sanctity is now fully implemented. New `Majik.Core.Tests/Players/PlayerHexproofTests.cs` (10 tests, all green) covers: default `HasHexproof = false`; registry add / remove / idempotent-duplicate-add; opponent's Lightning Bolt naming a non-hexproof player is legal (baseline); Sanctity on battlefield rejects opponent's Bolt naming controller with `RuleViolation 702.11`; controller's self-targeted Healing Salve still legal; LTB drops hexproof (Bolt re-legalises); two Sanctities stack idempotently, removing one preserves hexproof until the second leaves; opponent-controlled activated-ability targeting controller blocked; `Targeting.TargetLegality.IsLegal` rejects opponent spec and allows self spec when Sanctity is live. All 5348+ Core tests + 341 Server tests + 135 Bot tests still green. Coverage: Leyline of Sanctity row moves from "opening-hand alt-cost only" to "fully implemented".)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Cast-uncounterable primitive (CR 701.5b) + Emrakul, the Aeons Torn. `Spell.CannotBeCountered` (new `bool` on `Majik.Core/Spells/Spell.cs` + getter on `ISpell`) is the per-spell sentinel; `SpellCastFlow` stamps it `true` at cast time when the card carries a `KeywordAbility("Uncounterable")` marker (same scan shape as Annihilator / Flying / Indestructible — discoverability marker + cast-time read). `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` now returns `bool`: `false` short-circuits the pop when `ISpell.CannotBeCountered` is set so the uncounterable spell stays on the stack and resolves normally. Every counter primitive that pairs the pop with a "card → graveyard" tail (`Fx.Counter` + the three `CounterSpellFactory` shapes — plain "counter target spell", typed counter, "counter unless pay {N}") gates the tail on the bool so the card's tracking zone doesn't drift out of sync with the live stack. `ProtectionAbility` grows an optional `Func<ISpell, bool>? SpellPredicate` field so "protection from coloured spells" / future "from multicoloured" / "from non-Eldrazi" clauses can ship without inventing one-off colour-string tokens; `Rules.Protection.HasProtectionFromSpell(ICard, ISpell)` is the canonical read site (the legacy `HasProtectionFromColor` colour-string surface is untouched). `EmrakulTheAeonsTornFactory` ships the Rise-of-the-Eldrazi 15/15 Legendary Eldrazi {15}: `KeywordAbility("Flying")` + `KeywordAbility("Uncounterable")` markers + `KeywordAbility("Annihilator", arg: 6)` discoverability marker + the wired Annihilator 6 trigger via `AnnihilatorFactory.Build(card, n: 6, agentSelector)` (PR #496) + a `ProtectionAbility("coloured spells", spell => CardColors.GetColors(spell.Card).Count > 0)` predicate + a `SpellCastEvent`-gated cast trigger (`activeZones: [Stack]`) whose resolution enqueues an extra turn via `TurnManager.AddExtraTurn(controller)` (`TurnManager.AddExtraTurn` already existed — no new primitive). `Create(Player)` shape-only + `Create(Player, TurnManager?, TriggerManager?, Func<Player, IPlayerAgent?>?)` fully-wired overloads mirror Ulamog's wiring posture. Dispatches via `[CardName]` source-gen. Tests: `CannotBeCounteredTests` (6 facts — direct `RemoveFromStack` veto bool, `Fx.Counter` zone-tail skip, non-ISpell stack-object control, `SpellCastFlow` cast-time stamp) + `EmrakulTheAeonsTornFactoryTests` (6 facts — identity, NamedFactory dispatch, marker presence, protection predicate accepts colourless / rejects coloured, cast trigger enqueues extra turn). Coverage NamedFactory tier 300 → 301; previously deferred by PR #490 + PR #496 — now unblocked.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Opening-hand alt-cost surface + Leyline cycle — Void / Sanctity / Anguish / Lightning / Combustion. New `OpeningHandCheckEvent` (CR 103.5) published once per player by `GameDriver.RunGameAsync` AFTER the London mulligan loop resolves but BEFORE the turn loop begins, carrying a Player + an independent post-mulligan opening-hand snapshot in starting-player-first turn order. New `OpeningHandLeylineAlternativeCost` subscriber (CR 702.95) attached at game-start when an event bus is present: for each card in the published opening-hand snapshot carrying a `KeywordAbility("OpeningHandLeyline")` marker (constant `OpeningHandLeylineAlternativeCost.LeylineKeyword`), prompts the player's `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` with new `BotIntent.OpeningHandLeyline` (bit 25, upside-classified — default agent posture accepts) "Begin with [name] on the battlefield?" and on yes stamps `Card.WasCast = false` (CR 113.5 — Leyline is "put onto the battlefield", not cast, so Containment Priest's cast-gated rider sees the correct posture) before routing hand → battlefield via `ZoneService.MoveCard(controller: player)` so ETB triggers and `ReplacementBus` consumers fire normally. Multiple Leylines in the same opening hand each get their own prompt; a "no" on one does not skip the others. `LeylineOfTheVoidFactory` retrofit: adds the keyword marker and drops the "opening-hand alt-cost deferred" comment; the existing CR 614 opponent-graveyard-to-exile static replacement is unchanged. Four new sibling Leyline factories: `LeylineOfSanctityFactory` ({2}{W}{W} from M11 — opening-hand marker only; player-targeted hexproof rider deferred behind a future player-protection layer, same gap Veil of Summer's "you and permanents you control gain hexproof" already documents); `LeylineOfAnguishFactory` ({2}{B}{B} from MH3 — opening-hand marker only; controller-partitioned +1/+0 / -1/-0 power statics deferred behind a global-static Layer-7c surface); `LeylineOfLightningFactory` ({2}{R}{R} from MH3 — opening-hand marker only; "first spell each turn → 1 damage" trigger deferred behind a per-turn first-cast gate on the existing `Triggers.OnSpellCastByController` rail); `LeylineOfCombustionFactory` ({2}{R}{R} from M20 — opening-hand marker only; "opponent targets you or your permanent → 2 damage to that player" trigger deferred behind a targeting-resolution trigger predicate). All five Leylines dispatch through the `[CardName]` source-gen rail (`NamedCardFactory.Create("Leyline of Sanctity", owner)` etc.). New `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/OpeningHandLeylineTests.cs` (15 tests) covers: every Leyline carries the marker; identity / type / mana cost for each new factory; `NamedCardFactory` dispatch lights up; subscriber accepts → hand → battlefield; subscriber declines → stays in hand; multiple Leylines each prompted in event-snapshot order; non-Leyline hand cards aren't prompted (no spurious yes/no consumption); `WasCast` clears to false on the put-to-battlefield even when pre-stamped true; event-bus delivery; `GameDriver` publishes exactly one `OpeningHandCheckEvent` per player; opening-hand check fires AFTER mulligan resolution (post-bottom snapshot, 6-card hand after one mulligan + bottom-1). `GameDriver.cs` constructor stores `ZoneService` so the subscriber can be wired at game-start. `BotIntent.OpeningHandLeyline` upside-classification matches `CheatIntoPlay`'s default-accept posture in `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` default body. Coverage: +4 NamedFactory tier (Sanctity / Anguish / Lightning / Combustion). Infrastructure unblocks the rest of the Leyline cycle — Sanctity's hexproof / Anguish's power statics / Lightning's first-spell trigger / Combustion's targeting trigger and the remaining 5 unprinted Leylines slot into the same `KeywordAbility("OpeningHandLeyline")` marker with no further per-card wiring.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Annihilator (CR 702.86) primitive — `Majik.Core/Keywords/AnnihilatorFactory.cs`. `Build(Creature source, int n, Func<Player, IPlayerAgent?>? agentSelector)` returns a `TriggeredAbility` whose `EventTriggerCondition<CreatureAttacksEvent>` captures the live `e.DefendingPlayerOrPlaneswalker` (Player or planeswalker-controller) and whose effect loops N times: candidates pre-filtered to the defender's battlefield, agent-driven pick via the new `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromBattlefieldAsync(chooser, candidates, BotIntent.Removal)` (default-method on the interface — pre-agent first-candidate fallback so existing implementations don't break), illegal-pick guard (Zone == Battlefield + Controller == defender) demotes to first-candidate, route the sacrifice through `Fx.Sacrifice` (CR 701.16 / `ZoneMoveReason.Sacrifice` — bypasses Indestructible CR 702.12b + regeneration CR 701.15c). Short battlefield halts cleanly (sacrifice-as-many-as-available). Multiple Annihilator creatures attacking each fire independently per CR 702.86b — one trigger per source per attack event. Tokens sacrificed via the effect hit the graveyard and the next `TokensCeaseToExistCheck` SBA pass removes them per CR 110.7 / CR 704.5d (verified end-to-end in tests). `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromBattlefieldAsync` mirrors `ChooseFromHandAsync` (PR #483) shape — same default-impl + `ScriptedAgent.QueueFromBattlefield(Func / ICard?)` test helpers + same `BotIntent.Removal` posture. `KeywordAbility` extended with optional `int? Arg` (and `arg:` ctor parameter) so parameterised keywords (Annihilator N, future Ward N / Fading N / Vanishing N / Bushido N) carry the N value alongside the marker name; `Description` shows "Annihilator 2" when Arg is set, plain Keyword otherwise. `UlamogsCrusherFactory` ships the Rise-of-the-Eldrazi 8/8 Eldrazi {8}: discoverability `KeywordAbility("Annihilator", arg: 2)` + must-attack `KeywordAbility("AttacksEachCombat")` markers + the wired Annihilator trigger via `AnnihilatorFactory.Build(card, n: 2, agentSelector)`. Must-attack (CR 702.43 — combat-restriction wiring) shipped as marker only — same posture as Reality Smasher's non-mana Ward discard rider. Dispatches via `[CardName]` source-gen. Emrakul, the Aeons Torn still deferred — the cast-uncounterable surface (`Spell.CannotBeCountered`) doesn't exist yet in the engine; counter logic in `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` doesn't gate on a per-spell uncounterable flag. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Eldrazi cycle — Reality Smasher + Thought-Knot Seer + Ulamog, the Ceaseless Hunger. `RealitySmasherFactory` ships the OGW 5/5 Eldrazi {4}{C}: Trample + Haste + Ward keyword markers (printed non-mana 'discard a card' rider deferred — `WardEffect` helper exposed via `BuildWardEffect` with `ManaCost.Zero` and the discard cost surfaced as documentation constant `WardDiscardCost`; same mana-only-Ward-effect gap Kappa Cannoneer's {4} mana Ward sits behind). `ThoughtKnotSeerFactory` ships the OGW 4/4 Eldrazi {3}{C}: ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) over self-enter `CardMovedEvent` with a 1..1 'target opponent' `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.HandHate` — new flag bit at 1UL<<23 for opponent-hand-pick prompts, distinct from `Discard` because the chooser is not the card's owner); resolution reveals the target's hand, controller's `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromHandAsync(victim, nonlandHand, BotIntent.HandHate)` picks (deterministic first-nonland fallback, illegal-pick guard mirrors Liliana of the Veil's posture); exile via `ZoneService.MoveCard` when wired (`CardMovedEvent` publishes for Containment Priest / Tormod's Crypt subscribers), raw zone manipulation otherwise. LTB triggered ability (CR 603.6c / 603.10c) matches any FromZone == Battlefield, 1..1 'target opponent' request, `Fx.DrawCards(target, 1)` on resolution (`Player.MarkTriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` state-loss path preserved per CR 704.5b). Both triggers' active zones = Battlefield ('looks back' LTB semantics per CR 603.6d). `UlamogTheCeaselessHungerFactory` ships the BFZ Legendary Eldrazi {10} 10/10: Indestructible keyword marker (CR 702.12 — SBA reads via `CombatAbilities.HasIndestructible`); cast trigger (CR 603.6a / 603.10) over `SpellCastEvent` filtered to `ReferenceEquals(e.Spell.Card, card)` with `ActiveZones = Stack` matching Cascade self-cast posture (Crashing Footfalls / Living End), 2..2 'target permanent' `TargetRequest` tagged `BotIntent.Removal`, resolution exiles each chosen permanent (CR 701.21 — exile bypasses indestructible per CR 702.12b — `ZoneMoveReason.Other` implicit on raw zone path) with illegal-on-resolution Zone == Battlefield gate per CR 608.2b. Attack trigger (CR 508.1f / 603.1) over `Triggers.OnAttackSelf`-shaped predicate that captures the `DefendingPlayerOrPlaneswalker` as the victim (Player-only — planeswalker defender → no library so no-op); resolution loops 20 iterations exiling the top of `victim.Zones.Library` (short-library halts cleanly — CR 704.5b state-loss the next SBA pass handles, not this effect). All three dispatch via the `[CardName]` source-gen rail. Emrakul, the Aeons Torn intentionally deferred — Annihilator (CR 702.85) primitive doesn't exist yet (only mentioned in `KeywordAnalyzer.cs` classifier list); extra-turn primitive exists via `Game.ExtraTurnQueue` + `TurnManager.AddExtraTurn` but Annihilator gates the whole card's value posture, so deferred as a unit until the Annihilator-N primitive ships. Coverage NamedFactory tier 260 → 263; raw cover 27.4% → 27.4% (+13 distinct from cards-db refresh + 3 named); weighted 92.3% → 95.5%.)

**Latest origin/main:** Layer-4 type-strip primitive + Heliod devotion gate. New `Majik.Core/Effects/Layer4TypeStripEffect.cs` is a CR 205.2 / 613.1d Layer 4 (type-changing) continuous effect that conditionally strips `CardType.Creature` from a permanent while a controller-supplied `Func<bool>` predicate evaluates true. Source-anchored — `IsActive` gates on the source being on the battlefield, so the effect self-terminates on LTB; predicate is re-evaluated on every `ContinuousEffectsService.Compute` pass so live conditions (devotion, life totals, colour counts) drive the gate without re-registering. Layer-4-only — does NOT touch P/T / abilities / subtypes; stacks alongside other Layer 4 effects via timestamp + dependency sort (latest-timestamp wins per CR 613.7 when paired with a Layer 4 ADD-Creature effect like `KarnAnimateArtifactEffect` / `MutavaultFactory`'s animate). `HeliodSunCrownedFactory` gains a `(owner, triggers, effects)` overload that stamps the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` onto `Card.ActiveEffects` and registers `Layer4TypeStripEffect(card, () => ComputeDevotionToWhite(controller) < 5)` (clears PR #506's deferred type-strip — Heliod now correctly becomes a non-creature while devotion < 5 and recovers Creature when devotion hits 5+ via cast/LTB without effect re-registration). Tests: `Layer4TypeStripTests` covers strip-while-true / lifts-when-false / live-predicate / scope-to-source / source-LTB termination / stacks-with-other-Layer-4 (AddSubtype additive, KarnAnimate timestamp-wins) / null-arg guards. `HeliodSunCrownedTests` gains four scenarios: devotion-4 strips Creature, ineligible as a Doom-Blade target (creature-only predicate fails), devotion bumps to 5 restores Creature, devotion drops below 5 strips again. On top of Storm-pillar audit + Past in Flames (Sorcery {3}{R}, Innistrad). Audit confirms `Majik.Core/Keywords/StormHelper.cs` is the single shared on-cast trigger helper used by `BrainFreezeFactory` + `TendrilsOfAgonyFactory` (Storm-keyword cards); both use it identically (capture-into-closure storm-count read at trigger-evaluation time from `TurnState.SpellsCastByPlayer - 1`, then N `SpellCopier.PushCopyOfTopSpell` invocations on resolution). CR 702.40a "you may choose new targets" + copies-as-distinct-stack-objects gaps remain as documented (inherited from `SpellCopier`). `Manamorphose` already shipped with `ManamorphoseFactory` (Instant {1}{R/G}, "Add two mana in any combination of colors. Draw a card.") — audit confirmed no new `IPlayerAgent.ChooseManaColorsAsync` hook exists; existing caller-supplied `Func<Player, ManaColor[]>` colour-picker + deterministic `{R}{G}` default is the correct posture. New `PastInFlamesFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(controller, eventBus)` walks `controller.Zones.Graveyard.GetCards()`, filters via `HasType(CardType.Instant) || HasType(CardType.Sorcery)`, skips Past in Flames itself by name (its printed Flashback {4}{R} is the authoritative cost, not {3}{R}), and stamps `Card.GrantRuntimeFlashback` with each card's own `ManaCostValue` per CR 702.34. EOT cleanup via one-shot `StepStartedEvent`→`Cleanup` subscription clears every grant from the same resolution (CR 514.2 — same shape as `FlashbackFactory` + `SnapcasterMageFactory`). Printed Flashback {4}{R} on Past in Flames itself wired via `GetFlashbackAlternativeCost()` producing `FlashbackAlternativeCost(ManaCost.Parse("{4}{R}"))`. Bot-side discovery free via existing `RuntimeFlashbackAltCostProbe`. `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/PastInFlamesFactoryTests.cs` covers identity + dispatcher + grant-each-instant-sorcery + skips-creatures + skips-opponent-graveyard + skips-self + empty-graveyard no-op + EOT cleanup via bus + printed Flashback {4}{R} alt-cost. On top of Sorcery-speed activation gate (CR 117.1a / 307.5 — infrastructure PR). `ActivatedAbility` grows a `bool sorcerySpeed` ctor parameter + `IsSorcerySpeed` getter on `IActivatedAbility`; `ActivateAbilityAction` mirrors the cast-side `CastSpellAction.SorcerySpeedAvailable` pattern with a `(ability, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable)` overload (single-arg ctor defaults `SorcerySpeedAvailable: true` so the engine's hundreds of legacy activate-call sites are unaffected). `ActionValidator.ValidateActivateAbility` rejects sorcery-speed-only activations when the caller signals the window is closed, returning `RuleViolation("307.5", "activate-only-as-a-sorcery")`. `CardDefinitionFactory.BuildActivatedAbility` threads the JSON `sorcerySpeed` flag onto the runtime ability so JSON-defined cards (Walking Ballista's {4}: put counter) light up automatically. Nine named-factory retrofits flip the printed "Activate only as a sorcery" abilities through `sorcerySpeed: true`: `WishclawTalismanFactory`, `PriestOfFellRitesFactory`, `TasigurTheGoldenFangFactory`, `ColossusHammerFactory` / `SkullclampFactory` / `UmezawasJitteFactory` / `SwordOfFireAndIceFactory` / `SwordOfFeastAndFamineFactory` / `CoriSteelCutterFactory` (Equip {N}, CR 702.6a). `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/SorcerySpeedActivationTests.cs` covers the four-timing-windows matrix on Walking Ballista plus instant-speed-rejection on Wishclaw and Tasigur, the instant-speed-ability-bypass path, and the backward-compat default. Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #4 ("Activate only as a sorcery") moves from 9 blocked factories → 0 real (TirelessTracker false-positive remains). On top of Meltdown (Sorcery {X}{R} from Urza's Destiny — "Destroy each artifact with mana value X or less." `MeltdownFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(caster, allPlayers, x)` returns a single `IEffect` that snapshots every supplied player's battlefield, filters via `OfType<Card>()` + `HasType(CardType.Artifact)` + `ManaCostValue.TotalValue ≤ x`, HashSet-dedupes via `ReferenceEqualityComparer.Instance`, and routes each victim to its owner's graveyard via `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` (CR 701.7). Mirrors `WrathOfGodFactory.BuildResolveEffect`'s multi-player iteration shape with the Pernicious-Deed-style `mv ≤ X` predicate narrowed to Artifact only. X provenance: callers pass the resolved X directly (same simplification as Pernicious Deed / Engineered Explosives). Single-arg dispatcher path produces card shape only — no resolve effect, since X is unknown until cast. Indestructible bypass is the same lossy gap inherited from `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` — same as Slaughter Pact / Murderous Rider / Drown in the Loch / Embereth Shieldbreaker / Wrath of God) on top of Monastery Swiftspear (Creature — Human Monk {R} 1/2 from Khans of Tarkir + many reprints — Modern Burn / Izzet Prowess one-drop. `MonasterySwiftspearFactory.Create` ships the 1/2 Human Monk shape with the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as `EarthshakerKhenraFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory` / `GoblinRabblemasterFactory`) AND a Prowess `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.108) for shape-only keyword inspection alongside the prowess `TriggeredAbility` mechanic itself via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build(card, effects)` helper (sibling of `MonasteryMentorFactory`'s prowess wire-up — same `ContinuousEffectsService`-driven `ProwessPumpEffect`-on-Layer-7c posture). The `(owner, eventBus, triggers, effects)` overload registers the prowess trigger with the supplied `TriggerManager` so a controller-cast noncreature `SpellCastEvent` fires the prowess pump on Swiftspear (CR 702.108's "noncreature spell" predicate enforced by `ProwessFactory.Build`'s `EventTriggerCondition<SpellCastEvent>` — controller-match + `!HasType(CardType.Creature)`); `Creature.ActiveEffects` is wired to the supplied service so `Creature.Power` / `Creature.Toughness` reads flow through the layers compute. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both keyword markers but does NOT wire the trigger (no `ContinuousEffectsService` available at dispatch — shape-only posture, same as `MonasteryMentorFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory`). Distinct from Mentor's prowess wiring in one observable respect — Swiftspear surfaces a standalone `KeywordAbility("Prowess")` marker because the printed card line is literally "Prowess" (the vanilla keyword), whereas Mentor's reminder text is implicit in the trigger description and doesn't carry the marker. Vanilla-printed Prowess body — no ETB, no activated, no LTB clauses to defer; the only deferred surface is the same `TriggerManager` shape-vs-runtime split that every other prowess-bearing factory ships. Escape alt-cost (cast-from-graveyard + exile three other graveyard cards — CR 702.143) deferred — same gap as `UroTitanFactory`, blocked on the missing graveyard cast alt-cost + multi-card-exile additional-cost primitive. Clears top-20 #7) on top of The Meathook Massacre (Legendary Enchantment {X}{B}{B} from Innistrad: Midnight Hunt — X-cost ETB sweeper + symmetric dies-drain. Three triggered abilities wired: (1) ETB sweep reads X from `Card.PendingCastX` (stamped by `SpellCastFlow` at cast time, cleared on resolve so blink/copy can't reuse it — same shape as Chalice of the Void) and registers a `PumpUntilEndOfTurnEffect(-X, -X)` on every creature on every player's battlefield via each creature's own `ActiveEffects` service, mirroring `CountersSpellFactory.AllCreaturesPumpSpell`'s per-creature registration pattern; iteration goes through `allPlayersResolver?.Invoke()` or falls back to controller-only (same convention as Pernicious Deed). (2) Opponent-creature dies trigger: `CardMovedEvent` Battlefield→Graveyard filtered to `CardType.Creature` + controller ≠ Massacre's controller; effect calls `GainLife(1)` on the controller. (3) Own-creature dies trigger: same predicate inverted (controller = Massacre's controller); drains 1 life from every player supplied by an optional `opponentResolver`, defensively skipping the controller if the resolver returns the full `Game.Players` list — mirrors Sheoldred, the Apocalypse's resolver shape. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches all three triggers for shape; own-dies drain silently no-ops without a resolver, ETB sweep scans controller-only. On resolution, the targeted creature's controller (captured at trigger evaluation, not Nadu's controller — handles control-changing edge cases when that pathway lands) reveals the top of their library; land → battlefield (raw zone mutation; ZoneService routing for ETB / replacement effects deferred), non-land → hand. **Last updated:** 2026-05-24 (Companion deck-construction (CR 702.139) — `ICompanionRestriction` + `CompanionValidator`; Lurrus exposes its mv ≤ 2 predicate; runtime cast-from-outside-game half still deferred pending sideboard zone)
**Latest origin/main:** Companion deck-construction (CR 702.139 — infrastructure PR). New `ICompanionRestriction` interface (`Majik.Core/Rules/CompanionRestriction.cs`) declares `Description` + `IsSatisfiedBy(IEnumerable<ICard>)`; `CompanionValidator.Validate(companion, restriction, startingDeck)` aggregates into the existing `DeckValidationResult` shape, sibling to `DeckValidator`. `LurrusOfTheDreamDenFactory.CompanionRestriction` exposes a static singleton implementing "Each permanent card in your starting deck has mana value 2 or less" — it reuses `LurrusGraveyardCastGate.IsPermanentSpell` (CR 110.4a) for permanent-card identification and `MaxGraveyardCastManaValue` for the mv ceiling, so the deck-builder predicate and the runtime grave-cast predicate stay in sync. The runtime "cast from outside the game" half (companion-tax + once-per-game ledger) is still deferred — engine has no sideboard zone (`Majik.Core/Zones/ZoneType.cs`), so the cast pipeline has no source to draw from. `MechanicPrimitiveRegistry`'s `companion` entry narrows scope to runtime cast-from-outside (deck-construction half done). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/CompanionValidatorTests.cs` covers mv ≤ 2 legal deck, 3-mv permanent rejection, instant/sorcery exemption, predicate↔validator agreement, and null-arg guards. On top of Kicker additional-cost (CR 702.33 — infrastructure PR). `KickerAdditionalCost` ships as a real `IAdditionalCost` primitive (sibling of `BuybackAdditionalCost` / sacrifice-rider shape) carrying `(ICard card, ManaCost kickerCost)`; `Pay` drains the kicker mana via `Player.PayMana` and stamps a new `Card.WasKicked` sentinel (mirror on the resolving `Spell.WasKicked` set by `SpellCastFlow` when a `KickerAdditionalCost` is layered onto the cast). `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync` detects the cost in the existing `additionalCosts` merge loop, stamps the spell-level mirror, and appends a cleanup `Effect` to the wrapped effect list that clears `Card.WasKicked` after the spell's printed body resolves so the sentinel doesn't leak to copies / blinks / re-casts (CR 400.7 — new object on each zone change). Bot-side discovery via the new `KickerAltCostProbe` registered in `AlternativeCostProbeRegistry.CreateDefault` alongside Pitch / Delve / Overload / Cascade / Energy / Escape; mirrors `CascadeAltCostProbe`'s discovery-only posture (yields zero `IAlternativeCost` candidates because kicker is an additive cost, not an alt-cost) and exposes `KickerCostFor(card, caster)` + `BuildAdditionalCost(card)` for the bot's "should I pay the kicker?" decision. `KickerAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` recognises Burst Lightning as a {4}-kicker card. `BurstLightningFactory` retrofitted off the structural `wasKicked` parameter: `BuildSpellDefinition(card, resolver)` reads `Card.WasKicked` at resolve time and branches between 2 / 4 damage, and `BuildAdditionalCost(card)` returns the `{4}` kicker cost as a convenience builder. `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/KickerAdditionalCostTests.cs` covers the cost primitive (CanPay / Pay / mana-pool drain / Card.WasKicked stamp / failure atomicity), the cast-pipeline integration through Burst Lightning (not-kicked → 2 dmg, kicked → 4 dmg, post-resolve flag clear), and the probe discovery surface (hand / zone / owner pre-filter + registration in the default registry). Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #9 "Kicker alt-cost (CR 702.33)" drops to zero real blocking factories (SlickshotShowOff's docstring referenced Kicker as a sibling-shape comparison and is rewritten to point at Plot as its own pending cluster). On top of Sorcery-speed activation gate (CR 117.1a / 307.5 — infrastructure PR). `ActivatedAbility` grows a `bool sorcerySpeed` ctor parameter + `IsSorcerySpeed` getter on `IActivatedAbility`; `ActivateAbilityAction` mirrors the cast-side `CastSpellAction.SorcerySpeedAvailable` pattern with a `(ability, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable)` overload (single-arg ctor defaults `SorcerySpeedAvailable: true` so the engine's hundreds of legacy activate-call sites are unaffected). `ActionValidator.ValidateActivateAbility` rejects sorcery-speed-only activations when the caller signals the window is closed, returning `RuleViolation("307.5", "activate-only-as-a-sorcery")`. `CardDefinitionFactory.BuildActivatedAbility` threads the JSON `sorcerySpeed` flag onto the runtime ability so JSON-defined cards (Walking Ballista's {4}: put counter) light up automatically. Nine named-factory retrofits flip the printed "Activate only as a sorcery" abilities through `sorcerySpeed: true`: `WishclawTalismanFactory`, `PriestOfFellRitesFactory`, `TasigurTheGoldenFangFactory`, `ColossusHammerFactory` / `SkullclampFactory` / `UmezawasJitteFactory` / `SwordOfFireAndIceFactory` / `SwordOfFeastAndFamineFactory` / `CoriSteelCutterFactory` (Equip {N}, CR 702.6a). `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/SorcerySpeedActivationTests.cs` covers the four-timing-windows matrix on Walking Ballista plus instant-speed-rejection on Wishclaw and Tasigur, the instant-speed-ability-bypass path, and the backward-compat default. Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #4 ("Activate only as a sorcery") moves from 9 blocked factories → 0 real (TirelessTracker false-positive remains). On top of Meltdown (Sorcery {X}{R} from Urza's Destiny — "Destroy each artifact with mana value X or less." `MeltdownFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(caster, allPlayers, x)` returns a single `IEffect` that snapshots every supplied player's battlefield, filters via `OfType<Card>()` + `HasType(CardType.Artifact)` + `ManaCostValue.TotalValue ≤ x`, HashSet-dedupes via `ReferenceEqualityComparer.Instance`, and routes each victim to its owner's graveyard via `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` (CR 701.7). Mirrors `WrathOfGodFactory.BuildResolveEffect`'s multi-player iteration shape with the Pernicious-Deed-style `mv ≤ X` predicate narrowed to Artifact only. X provenance: callers pass the resolved X directly (same simplification as Pernicious Deed / Engineered Explosives). Single-arg dispatcher path produces card shape only — no resolve effect, since X is unknown until cast. Indestructible bypass is the same lossy gap inherited from `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` — same as Slaughter Pact / Murderous Rider / Drown in the Loch / Embereth Shieldbreaker / Wrath of God) on top of Monastery Swiftspear (Creature — Human Monk {R} 1/2 from Khans of Tarkir + many reprints — Modern Burn / Izzet Prowess one-drop. `MonasterySwiftspearFactory.Create` ships the 1/2 Human Monk shape with the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as `EarthshakerKhenraFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory` / `GoblinRabblemasterFactory`) AND a Prowess `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.108) for shape-only keyword inspection alongside the prowess `TriggeredAbility` mechanic itself via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build(card, effects)` helper (sibling of `MonasteryMentorFactory`'s prowess wire-up — same `ContinuousEffectsService`-driven `ProwessPumpEffect`-on-Layer-7c posture). The `(owner, eventBus, triggers, effects)` overload registers the prowess trigger with the supplied `TriggerManager` so a controller-cast noncreature `SpellCastEvent` fires the prowess pump on Swiftspear (CR 702.108's "noncreature spell" predicate enforced by `ProwessFactory.Build`'s `EventTriggerCondition<SpellCastEvent>` — controller-match + `!HasType(CardType.Creature)`); `Creature.ActiveEffects` is wired to the supplied service so `Creature.Power` / `Creature.Toughness` reads flow through the layers compute. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both keyword markers but does NOT wire the trigger (no `ContinuousEffectsService` available at dispatch — shape-only posture, same as `MonasteryMentorFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory`). Distinct from Mentor's prowess wiring in one observable respect — Swiftspear surfaces a standalone `KeywordAbility("Prowess")` marker because the printed card line is literally "Prowess" (the vanilla keyword), whereas Mentor's reminder text is implicit in the trigger description and doesn't carry the marker. (2) Opponent-creature dies trigger: `CardMovedEvent` Battlefield→Graveyard filtered to `CardType.Creature` + controller ≠ Massacre's controller; effect calls `GainLife(1)` on the controller. (3) Own-creature dies trigger: same predicate inverted (controller = Massacre's controller); drains 1 life from every player supplied by an optional `opponentResolver`, defensively skipping the controller if the resolver returns the full `Game.Players` list — mirrors Sheoldred, the Apocalypse's resolver shape. On resolution, the targeted creature's controller (captured at trigger evaluation, not Nadu's controller — handles control-changing edge cases when that pathway lands) reveals the top of their library; land → battlefield (raw zone mutation; ZoneService routing for ETB / replacement effects deferred), non-land → hand. Mode 1 pulls 2 cards from the *target player's* library — distinct from Cryptic Command's controller-only draw. Picks go to hand; remainder re-bottoms shuffled via `Random.Shared` (CR 701.20a). Iteration is enum-driven so Battle (MoM+) lights up automatically once `CardType.Battle` is added. Each activation closes over its own resolve-time creature pick + delayed sac trigger so multiple activations in the same turn each sacrifice their cheated-in creature independently. v1 deterministic first-creature-in-hand pick (auto-accepts the "you may" when a candidate exists — same shape as Through the Breach / Aether Vial / Goblin Lackey). Hand → battlefield routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a); Haste via `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (CR 613.1c Layer 6) plus `HasSummoningSickness=false` for attack-declaration (CR 702.10b — the EOT-scoped grant is observationally equivalent to the printed no-duration wording since the creature is sacrificed at the same boundary). Resolve picks the first creature in caster's hand (v1 deterministic "you may" auto-accept like Aether Vial), moves it hand → battlefield through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a), grants Haste EOT via `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (CR 613.1c Layer 6) and clears summoning sickness (CR 702.10b), then registers a one-shot `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) that sacrifices the placed creature at the start of the next end step (CR 500.4 / CR 701.16 — controller's battlefield → owner's graveyard, fence-checked with `e.Timestamp > resolvedAt` to skip the current end step). The closure runs two independent statements: (1) CR 305.2 — bumps the controller's per-turn land-drop cap by reading `LandDropTracker.MaxLandDropsThisTurn(caster)` and writing `current + 1` via `SetMaxLandDropsThisTurn`. (2) CR 121.1 — `Fx.DrawCards(caster, 1)` (empty-library path stamps the CR 704.5b / CR 120.3 `TriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` loss flag via `Fx.DrawCards`'s internal `MarkTriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` call). **`LandDropTracker` "add" surface audit**: as of PR #447 (LandDropTracker introduction) the tracker exposes only `SetMaxLandDropsThisTurn` (absolute setter) + `MaxLandDropsThisTurn` (reader) — there is no additive `AddExtraLandDrop` / `BumpMaxLandDropsThisTurn` method. Explore (and Azusa / Exploration / Oracle of Mul Daya when they ship) read current + write current+1; stack-safe (bump applied at resolution) + turn-safe (`LandDropTracker.ResetTurn` clears the per-turn max on every turn change via `TurnDriver`, so multi-turn carry-over isn't possible). Multi-Explore stacking is additive (each cast reads the post-prior cap). `BuildResolveEffect` accepts a null tracker for shape-only test paths — the draw still runs, bump is skipped. The printed "you may" is auto-accepted at v1 (same posture as Sneak Attack / Through the Breach pre-prompt). Amulet Titan ramp cantrip — gas + extra land drop in one card. |
| Eternal Witness | Creature | TBD | Creature — Human Shaman {1}{G}{G} 2/1 (Fifth Dawn + many reprints). `EternalWitnessFactory.Create` ships the 2/1 Human Shaman shape plus a single ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) wired via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a bespoke 1..1 "target card in your graveyard" `TargetRequest` (ANY card type — distinct from Animate Dead's creature-only graveyard filter). `LegalCandidates` seeded from `controller.Zones.Graveyard.GetCards()` at trigger-creation time; production callers refresh via `TriggeredAbility.SetChosenTargets` from an agent prompt before resolution (same pattern as Snapcaster Mage). On resolution the effect honours `ChosenTargets[0][0]` when present, otherwise falls back deterministically to the first card in the controller's graveyard (single-arg dispatcher path — mirrors Wishclaw Talisman's tutor fallback / Tasigur's agent-null fallback). Resolution-time legality gate (CR 608.2b — must still be in controller's graveyard; cards that left between trigger creation + resolution fizzle cleanly). Empty graveyard → clean no-op. Move Graveyard → Hand routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied (publishes `CardMovedEvent` so any zone-change triggers fire per CR 603.6a / CR 701.20); otherwise direct-zone mutation. "You may" auto-accepted at v1 — same posture as Tireless Tracker / Phlage / Snapcaster Mage's ETB grant. Card-type agnostic (Instant / Sorcery / Creature / Land all returnable — verified via theory test). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the trigger shape without TriggerManager registration; (owner, zoneService, eventBus, triggers) overload wires bus-driven firing + ZoneService-routed return. Modern Yawgmoth / Junk / Bant Eldrazi value-recursion staple |
| Esper Sentinel | Creature | TBD | Creature — Human Soldier {W} 1/1 (Modern Horizons 2). `EsperSentinelFactory.Create` ships the 1/1 Human Soldier shape + a single triggered ability over `SpellCastEvent` (CR 603.1) that fires on each opponent's FIRST noncreature spell each turn. Per-player first-cast tracking uses a `Dictionary<Player, int>` closure keyed by caster reference (mirrors Ledger Shredder's per-turn count shape but keyed per opponent so multi-opponent games surface each opponent's own first-cast independently). Predicate filters out (a) the controller's own spells ("an opponent casts") and (b) creature spells ("noncreature spell"); the exact transition from 0→1 fires, subsequent casts that turn don't retrigger (CR 603.2 — trigger only fires when the condition becomes true). On resolve, X is recomputed at resolve time as `owner.Zones.Battlefield.GetCards().Count(c => c.HasType(CardType.Creature) && c.Controller == owner)` per CR 608.2 (X = 0 is treated as a trivial auto-pay per CR 117.5 → no draw). The opponent caster is consulted via `Player.PayMana(ManaCost.Zero.AddGenericCost(x))` — same auto-pay-from-pool posture as Cursecatcher / Daze / Mana Leak (`ChooseYesNoAsync` agent prompt deferred — same queue as the Pact cycle / Cursecatcher's "unless pay" clause). On failure the controller draws 1 via `Fx.DrawCards`. Per-turn closure is reset on `TurnStartedEvent` (CR 500.1). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the trigger shape without `TriggerManager` / bus wiring; `(owner, eventBus, triggers)` overload wires per-player count reset + trigger registration. Modern white prison / Hammer Time / Death and Taxes card-advantage staple. |

| Gisela, Blade of Goldnight | Creature | TBD | Legendary Creature — Angel {4}{R}{W}{W} 5/5 (Avacyn Restored). `GiselaBladeOfGoldnightFactory.Create` ships the 5/5 Legendary Angel shape with Flying + First Strike + Lifelink `KeywordAbility` markers (CR 702.9 / 702.7 / 702.15 — combat helpers read these directly, same wiring as Atraxa's evergreen block). Two replacement clauses register against the supplied `ReplacementBus`: (1) **doubling** — single `DamageDoubleReplacement` (`Majik.Core/Effects/`) gated on Gisela being on the battlefield AND target being an opponent or an opponent's permanent (shared with Angrath's via `AngrathsMaraudersFactory.TargetIsOpponentOrTheirPermanent`). Gisela's printed text omits the "you control" source restriction Angrath's carries, so the predicate has no source-controller gate — every damage intent aimed at an opponent doubles regardless of source ownership (CR 614). (2) **halving** — single new `DamageHalveRoundedUpReplacement` primitive (`Majik.Core/Effects/`) gated on target being the controller or controller's permanent (via local `TargetIsControllerOrTheirPermanent` helper). The halving primitive rewrites `intent.Amount` to `(amount + 1) / 2` — "prevent half that damage, rounded up" (CR 615); 1→1, 2→1, 3→2, 4→2, 5→3, 7→4, 10→5. Both clauses gate on `card.Zone == ZoneType.Battlefield` so blink / bounce auto-suspends both. Controller is read live from `Card.Controller` for both clauses so Mind Control / Threaten repoints them. Multi-source interactions: Furnace of Rath + Gisela on opponent-side damage quadruples (Furnace 2x → Gisela 2x = 4x); on controller-side damage nets ~1x (Furnace 2x → Gisela halve rounded up cancels out — 4 → 8 → 4). | Inquisitor's Flail | Artifact | TBD | Artifact — Equipment {2} (Innistrad). `InquisitorsFlailFactory.Create` ships the {2} Equipment shape + Equip {2} via the shared `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive with the Puresteel zero-equip cost-provider hook — sorcery-speed gate + target-creature-you-control candidate gathering + attach-on-resolve all encapsulated (same wiring as Bone Saw / Colossus Hammer / Skullclamp / Sword of Fire and Ice). Two-overload shape: single-arg `Create(Player)` (shape-only dispatcher path — no `ReplacementBus` wiring; combat-damage doubling no-ops) + `Create(owner, ReplacementBus?)` for fully-wired behaviour. Both printed damage-doubling replacements (CR 614 / CR 510.1c) ship via two `DamageDoubleReplacement` registrations on the supplied bus: (1) **source-side** — "if equipped creature would deal combat damage, it deals double that damage instead" gates on `intent.Source == card.AttachedTo` AND `intent.IsCombatDamage`; (2) **target-side** — "if a source would deal combat damage to equipped creature, it deals double that damage instead" gates on `intent.TargetCreature == card.AttachedTo` AND `intent.IsCombatDamage`. Both gate on the Flail being on the battlefield AND currently attached, so detach / blink / bounce automatically suspends doubling without explicit deregistration. Non-combat damage paths (spells, ping abilities) leave `DamageIntent.IsCombatDamage` false and the gates short-circuit (no doubling of Lightning Bolt's targeted damage to the equipped creature, no doubling of the equipped creature's `{T}` ping ability). `DamageDoubleReplacement` is the generic "double a combat-damage intent matching a per-side predicate" replacement primitive — future retrofits (Furnace of Rath, Gisela the Broken Blade, Boros Charm's third mode) can lean on the same shape with their own gates |

(3) `{1}{R}, Sacrifice Roiling Vortex: deal 3 damage to any target` activated ability (CR 602) with `ManaCostCost("{1}{R}")` + `AdditionalCost.Sacrifice(card)`; resolution dispatches damage via `OracleSpellBinder.DealDamage` (Player / Creature target). `AdditionalCost.Sacrifice` is the v1 no-op stub (same gap as Relic of Progenitus / Nihil Spellbomb) so the activation does not actually graveyard Vortex in v1. (4) "Players can't gain life" static (CR 614 / CR 119.6) registers a new `LifeGainIntent` lambda replacement on the supplied `ReplacementBus` rewriting every gain to `Amount = 0`. Players opt into the bus via the new `Player.AttachReplacementBus`; `Player.GainLife` consults the bus when attached and otherwise takes the direct mutation path it has always taken (backward-compat). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both triggers + activated ability for shape but omits all service wiring (mirrors Valakut's posture); use `RoilingVortexFactory.Create(owner, triggers, replacements, allPlayersResolver)` for fully-wired behaviour. Clears top-20 #11. |

### Template-covered (notable)

Cards implemented through generic or bespoke templates without a named factory. Selected highest-value Modern staples:

- Lightning Bolt — `Damage/DamageAnyTargetTemplate`
- Lava Spike — `Damage/DamagePlayerTemplate`
- Lava Dart — `Damage/DamageAnyTargetTemplate` (flashback handled by KeywordBinder)
- Skewer the Critics — `Damage/DamagePlayerTemplate`
- Mana Tithe — `Counter/CounterUnlessPayTemplate`
- Spell Pierce — `Counter/CounterUnlessPayTemplate` (regex broadened to recognize the "noncreature" type qualifier; pay rider consults the target spell's controller's mana pool)
- Counterspell — `Counter/CounterTargetSpellTemplate`
- Boros Charm — `Bespoke/StriveTemplate`-adjacent
- Boltwave — `Damage/DealsNDamageEachOpponentTemplate`
- Goblin Guide — vanilla keyword binding (Haste + reveal-trigger TBD)
- Eidolon of the Great Revel — triggered-ability binder (cheap-spell pattern)
- Monastery Swiftspear — Prowess keyword (`Keywords/ProwessFactory`)
- Fulminator Mage — `FulminatorMageFactory` (sacrifice-self → destroy nonbasic land, Wasteland-shape twin minus tap)
- Force of Vigor — `Destroy/DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentTemplate` + pitch cost
- Fatal Push — `Destroy/DestroyCreatureCmcLimitTemplate`
- Thoughtseize — `Bespoke/ThoughtseizePatternTemplate`
- Inquisition of Kozilek — `Bespoke/InquisitionOfKozilekPatternTemplate` (nonland + mv ≤ 3 cap; no life cost)
- Expressive Iteration — `Bespoke/ExpressiveIterationTemplate`
- Brainstorm — `Bespoke/BrainstormTemplate` (draw 3, put 2 from hand on top of library; v1 deterministic return order)
- Malevolent Rumble — `Bespoke/MalevolentRumblePatternTemplate` + `MalevolentRumbleFactory` (named-factory parallel: `Create(owner)` ships the Sorcery {1}{G} shape, `BuildResolveEffect(caster)` mirrors the template's reveal-4 / first-permanent-to-hand / rest-to-graveyard / `TokenFactory.CreateEldraziSpawn` body; `NamedCardFactory` dispatches the printed name to the factory so bot-deck loaders / shape-only fixtures bypass the Scryfall round-trip).
- Fetch lands cycle (10 of them) — `OracleLandActivatedAbilityBinder`
- Shock lands (Overgrown Tomb + cycle starts) — `Effects/ShockLandReplacement`
- Sacred Foundry, Steam Vents — shock-land binding
- Undying creatures (Young Wolf, Strangleroot Geist, Butcher Ghoul, Geralf's Messenger) — `Keywords/UndyingFactory`
- Ashiok, Dream Render — loyalty-ability binder (mill -1)
- Fable of the Mirror-Breaker — Saga chapter binder (partial)
- The Legend of Roku — Saga chapter binder (partial)

Cast-as-Adventure from hand routes through `SpellCastFlow` with the Adventure mana cost (sorcery-speed gate enforced for sorcery Adventures via `IsLegalInContext`); on resolve `Spell.PostResolutionZoneOverride = Exile` re-routes the card to exile (CR 715.3d) instead of the printed-type default (battlefield for creatures), and `AdventureAlternativeCost.OnResolved` stamps a runtime exile-cast grant via `Card.GrantRuntimeExileCast` so the owner may cast the creature face from exile via `ExileCastAlternativeCost` for the printed cost. Bonecrusher Giant / Murderous Rider / Embereth Shieldbreaker factories rewired through this surface. |

Attaches a `KeywordAbility "Modular N"` marker, registers `EntersWithCountersReplacement(card, N)` against the supplied ReplacementBus so the ETB +1/+1 stamp routes through `ZoneService` → `CountersService.Add` (Hardened Scales bumps apply — PR #494), and wires the Battlefield → Graveyard death trigger that snapshot-reads the dying permanent's +1/+1 counter total off the graveyard object (Undying-shape) and routes the bestowal through `CountersService.Add` onto the controller-side first-artifact-creature target. The "you may" rider consults `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.CardAdvantage)` — default = yes. Deferred: per-target prompt (deterministic v1 pick), cross-battlefield target enumeration (controller-only scan in v1). | Arcbound Ravager, Arcbound Worker, Arcbound Stinger |
| Dredge | Done | `Keywords/DredgeFactory.cs` (CR 702.52) — `Build(source, n, replacementBus?, eventBus?)` attaches a `KeywordAbility "Dredge"` marker (Arg = N) plus a graveyard-anchored `LambdaReplacement<DrawCardIntent>` on the supplied `ReplacementBus`. The replacement gates on (a) would-draw player == source.Owner, (b) source.Zone == Graveyard (CR 702.52a), (c) owner.Library.Count >= N (CR 702.52b), (d) agent yes (`IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` with `BotIntent.CardAdvantage`). On yes: mill N via `MillAction.Apply`, return source Graveyard → Hand, cancel the underlying draw (return null). New `Effects/DrawCardIntent.cs` intent + `Fx.DrawCards` routes each individual draw through the player's attached bus when present. No-bus shape-only path attaches the marker without firing a replacement. | Life from the Loam (Dredge 3), Stinkweed Imp (Dredge 5), Golgari Grave-Troll (Dredge 6) |
| Persist | Done | `Keywords/PersistFactory.cs` (CR 702.79) — promoted out of `KitchenFinksFactory` once Murderous Redcap + Glen Elendra Archmage joined the roadmap (Modular-promotion shape, negative-counter mirror of Undying). Attaches a `KeywordAbility "Persist"` marker + the Battlefield → Graveyard death trigger with the `InterveningIf` "no -1/-1 counters at death" gate (CR 603.4). Resolution body raw-moves Graveyard → Battlefield (UndyingFactory-shape — no `CardMovedEvent` re-publish), clears the counter bag (CR 121.2), then adds exactly one `CounterType.MinusOneMinusOne` via `CountersService.Add(replacements)` for symmetry with the Undying / Modular return-side counter route. ActiveZones = {Battlefield, Graveyard} so the trigger evaluates after the death zone-move. | Kitchen Finks, Murderous Redcap, Glen Elendra Archmage |

"The Mercenary" referent captured via a per-Class spawned-token holder. ### Infra / build

| Surface | Status | File |
|---|---|---|
| `NamedCardFactory` source-gen dispatch | Done | `Majik.Core.SourceGen/NamedCardFactoryGenerator.cs` — Roslyn `IIncrementalGenerator` scans every `[CardName("...")]`-attributed factory class and emits the dispatch switch into a partial `NamedCardFactory.CreateGenerated(string, Player)` method. Replaces the hand-maintained 296-arm switch (3581 → 97 lines) so adding a card is now "drop a factory file with `[CardName("Foo")]`" — no edits to the shared dispatch file required, eliminating the merge-conflict hotspot. Diagnostics: `MJK001` (duplicate `[CardName]` across factories — error), `MJK002` (factory missing `Create(Player)` overload — error). Generated file lives at `Majik.Core/obj/$(Configuration)/$(TargetFramework)/generated/Majik.Core.SourceGen/…` (EmitCompilerGeneratedFiles=true). |
| `scaffold-factory` CLI subcommand | Done | `Majik.Console/Commands/ScaffoldFactoryCommand.cs` + `ScaffoldFactoryGenerator.cs` + `ScryfallNamedLookup.cs` — `dotnet run --project Majik.Console -- scaffold-factory "<Card Name>" [--out <path>] [--force]` pulls the card via `ICardRepository.GetByName` against `cards.db`, falls back to `https://api.scryfall.com/cards/named?exact=…` when unknown locally, then writes a starter `<Slug>Factory.cs` into `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` with the `[CardName]` attribute, oracle-text docstring, ctor pre-filled from Scryfall (`Creature`/`Instant`/`Sorcery`/`Enchantment`/`Artifact`/`Land`/`Planeswalker` chosen via the same primary-type preference as `ScryfallCardFactory.PickPrimaryType`), and `// TODO: resolve body` markers. Coverage tier is reported up front via `CoverageClassifier` and prompts before overwriting an already-covered card (skip with `--force`). Refuses to overwrite an existing file without `--force`. Goal: cut per-card writing cost by ~50% by eliminating the boilerplate. |
| `ScryfallCardFactory` golden-snapshot suite | Done | `Majik.Core.Tests/Snapshots/` — 200-card golden-snapshot regression suite for the full `ScryfallCardFactory.Create` pipeline. Each fixture card has a bundled `CardEntity` row (`card-data/<slug>.json`) and a deterministic snapshot of the post-binder output (`snapshots/<slug>.json`): card name + mana cost + primary/super/sub types + P/T + loyalty + per-shape ability counts/descriptions (keyword markers, mana abilities, activated/triggered/static — sorted alphabetically) + ReplacementBus rider counts. Reflection over `ReplacementBus._effects` introspects the riders the binders pushed for snapshotting; no production code touched. Re-record via `SNAPSHOT_RECORD=1 dotnet test` against a local `cards.db`. Selection spans all binders: 30 lands (basics + shocks + fetches + horizon + Tron + manlands), 30 keyword creatures, 20 replacement-effect cards, 7 multi-face, 49 templated spells, 49 named-factory cards, + 15 fill from `[CardName]` registry. Three sanity tests confirm fixture size = 200, every card builds a non-empty snapshot, and two builds of the same card produce byte-identical output. |
| Fluent `CardDef` DSL | Done | `Majik.Core/CardData/Definitions/{CardDef,CardDefBuilder,ResolveBuilder,CardDefRuntime}.cs` — fluent code-side card builder for simple factories: a factory's `Create(Player)` collapses to `public static CardDef Define() => CardDef.Creature(...).WithSubtype(...).WithKeyword(...);` plus an optional one-line typed shim. The source generator detects `static CardDef Define()` and synthesizes the dispatch arm calling `CardDefRuntime.Build(Define(), owner)` directly when no `Create(Player)` is declared (Define-only path), or routes through the typed shim when present. Resolve bodies (`.Resolve(c => c.DealDamage(3).To(TargetKind.AnyTarget))`) materialize into the engine's existing `IEffect` list via inline builders; effects-primitive library integration is gated by `TODO(effects-primitives)` markers awaiting `Majik.Core/Effects/Primitives/`. 9 pilot factories migrated (BadgermoleCub, GurmagAngler, PhoenixOfAsh, OrcishBowmasters, Grist, SolRing, MishrasWorkshop, ForceOfWill, ForceOfNegation, ForceOfVigor) — average factory drops from ~50 LOC to ~10 LOC of fluent calls. |

Adds `Majik.Core/Players/Agents/PlayerAgentTargetingExtensions.cs` for the factory-facing `ChooseTargetAsync(ctx, description, candidates, intent)` + plural variant (null-agent path falls through deterministically — preserves the v1 graceful-degrade posture for shape-only tests). Extends `HeuristicBotAgent.Score` with intent-aware ranking for `Counter` (most-expensive stack spell), `Bounce` / `Discard` / `Mill` (most-expensive opponent permanent), and `Removal` / `Burn` / `Buff` / `CombatTrick` (power-weighted creature scoring). 11-factory pilot retrofit (Solitude, Earthshaker Khenra, Phlage, Murderous Rider, Bonecrusher Giant, Drown in the Loch, Swords to Plowshares, Dismember, Vapor Snag, Reflector Mage, Spell Queller) — each adds `CandidateGatherer` + `Intent` so the bot agent ranks against the live board instead of falling through the empty-list label sniffer. Decks containing unimplemented cards now play to completion instead of crashing or no-op-looping; the portal surfaces the unimplemented-card events as a "coverage gap" notice.
