## Coverage by archetype

- **Burn** — Saturated. Lightning Bolt, Lava Spike, Lava Dart, Skewer the Critics, Boros Charm, Eidolon of the Great Revel, Goblin Guide, Monastery Swiftspear (`MonasterySwiftspearFactory` — 1/2 Human Monk {R} with Haste + Prowess `KeywordAbility` markers + prowess `TriggeredAbility` via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build` helper), Rift Bolt, Searing Blaze, Lightning Helix, Sunscorched Desert (`SunscorchedDesertFactory` — Land from HOU; unconditional ETB-tapped replacement via `EntersTappedReplacement` on the supplied `ReplacementBus` + ETB triggered ability dealing 1 damage to any target via `SearingBlazeFactory.DealDamageWithPlaneswalker` + `{T}: Add {C}`), Roiling Vortex (`RoilingVortexFactory` — {R} Enchantment with the upkeep 1-to-each-player + free-cast 3-to-the-caster + {1}{R}/sac 3-to-any-target activated + "players can't gain life" replacement; closes the Modern Burn sideboard hate slot against UWx control and lifegain decks) all in. ~100%.
- **Death's Shadow** — Mid-high. Thoughtseize, Fatal Push, Snapcaster Mage, Stubborn Denial, Death's Shadow itself (CDA P/T scaled by controller life — Layer 7a) all in. Mishra's Bauble in. Temur Battle Rage absent. ~60%.
- **Murktide / Izzet Tempo** — High. Murktide Regent done, Counterspell done, Snapcaster Mage done, Lightning Bolt done, Expressive Iteration done, Ledger Shredder done, Consider done, Spell Pierce done, Subtlety done. Monastery Swiftspear done (`MonasterySwiftspearFactory` — Modern Izzet Prowess one-drop, Haste + Prowess wiring via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build` helper). Sprite Dragon done (`SpriteDragonFactory` — Modern Izzet Prowess two-drop, Flying + cast-noncreature +1/+1 counter accumulator; same predicate shape as `ProwessFactory` but persistent counters instead of one-turn pump). Soul-Scar Mage done (Prowess 1-drop that converts noncombat damage from controller-owned sources to -1/-1 counters on opponent creatures — first `IReplacementEffect<DamageIntent>` in the engine; pairs with Swiftspear and Sprite Dragon as the Izzet Prowess cheap-creature trio). Cori-Steel Cutter done (`CoriSteelCutterFactory` — {1}{R} Equipment from Tarkir: Dragonstorm; static "+1/+1 + trample + haste" via paired `AttachedBoostEffect` registrations at Layers 7c / 6, Flurry — second-spell-each-turn trigger spawning a 1/1 white Monk token with prowess + auto-attaching; Equip {1}{R}). Demilich done (`DemilichFactory` — {3}{U}{U}{U} 4/3 Zombie Wizard with Flying; `CostReductionAbility(totalReducer)` counts instant+sorcery cards in caster's graveyard for {U}-less-per-card discount floored at the three blue pips per CR 117.7c; on-cast `SpellCastEvent` self-trigger exiles two instants/sorceries from caster's graveyard with agent-prompt-via-`ChooseFromPileAsync` or deterministic first-two fallback). ~95%.

  **Izzet Tempo audit (this PR) — deferred riders enumerated, not fixed:**
  - `LedgerShredderFactory` — `SurveilEvent` shipped (Events/SurveilEvent.cs + Fx.Surveil bus publish + Triggers.OnSurveil) and the surveil-self counter trigger now subscribes to it directly. First-spell-each-turn counter still increments on every controller `SpellCastEvent`, including Ledger Shredder's own cast (intentional, but callers exercising the trigger manually must publish a `SpellCastEvent` for the first spell too).
  - `MurktideRegentFactory` — +1/+1 counters land via the ETB effect rather than a CR 122.1g "as it enters" replacement (no general 122.1g infrastructure). Delve count is stamped on `Card.PendingDelveExiledCount` rather than threaded through a generalised X-counter ETB framework. ETB-exile counts alongside delve exiles in the counter total (matches the v1 spec but not strict CR 122.1g ordering).
  - `ConsiderFactory` — surveil decision quality lives in registered `IPlayerAgent` impl; deterministic fallback sends peeked card to graveyard. No deferred rider on the printed effect itself.
  - `ExpressiveIterationTemplate` — deterministic 3-way split (first→hand, second→bottom, third→exile) instead of player-choice; needs a "choose-among" decision shape on the agent. "You may play the exiled card this turn" rider absent — requires the cast-from-exile-this-turn permission tracker (CR 118.10) shared by Lurrus / Den of the Bugbear / similar.
  - `ArclightPhoenixFactory` — graveyard-resident trigger's `activeZones = {Graveyard}` relies on `TriggerManager` respecting graveyard triggers; bus-driven firing requires the `(owner, bus, triggers)` overload. Cast count is captured at begin-combat-step time — spells cast after the trigger resolves don't retroactively requalify the Phoenix the same turn. "You may" prompt routes through `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` (`BotIntent.Reanimate | BotIntent.CardAdvantage`) when an agent is supplied, auto-accept otherwise.
  - `DemilichFactory` (new) — flashback-style cast-from-graveyard-by-exiling-four clause from the real Innistrad print is deliberately NOT shipped (this v1 follows MODERN_COVERAGE row #10's engine-friendly spec). Attack-trigger copy-from-graveyard clause from the real print is also not in scope. On-cast exile is non-optional (no "may"). Agent prompt for the two graveyard cards uses `BotIntent.None`.
- **Mono-Green Tron** — High. Ancient Stirrings, Sylvan Scrying, Wurmcoil Engine done. Karn Liberated done. Karn, the Great Creator done. Tron lands (Urza's Mine + Tower + Power-Plant) done with the conditional {2} mana ability. ~70%.
- **Living End / Crashing Footfalls cascade** — High. Cascade keyword done (`Keywords/CascadeAction.cs`) + Crashing Footfalls shipped (#219). Living End shipped (this PR) with both the Cascade trigger and the resolve chain (per-player mass-exile-grave + sacrifice-creatures + mass-reanimate; ETB triggers fire on reanimated permanents via PR #174 plumbing). Suspend itself is done (#183). ~75%.
- **Rakdos Scam** — High. Grief done (#205, mirrors Solitude evoke + ETB pattern). Fury done (mirrors Solitude/Grief). Ragavan, Nimble Pilferer done (combat-damage Treasure + exile + may-cast EOT grant; Dash deferred). Dauthi Voidwalker done (Shadow + opponent-grave→exile-with-void-counter replacement effect + {2},{T},remove-void-counter activated cast-from-exile via CastFromExileAlternativeCost; EOT "this turn" timing on the cast permission deferred). Liliana of the Veil done, Fatal Push done, Thoughtseize done. ~75%.
- **Yawgmoth combo** — High. Yawgmoth done. Yawgmoth's Will done (Sorcery {2}{B} — stamps Card.RuntimeGraveyardCastCost on every card in controller's graveyard + EOT-expirable grave→exile ZoneMoveIntent replacement; covers the "play cards from your graveyard" + "exile instead of graveyard" oracle clauses). Undying engine done — Young Wolf (1/1 {G} Wolf, `YoungWolfFactory`), Strangleroot Geist (2/1 {G}{G} Spirit with a Haste rider, `StrangleRootGeistFactory`), and Geralf's Messenger (3/2 {B}{B}{B} Zombie with the unconditional enters-tapped replacement + ETB target-opponent-loses-2-life trigger, `GeralfsMessengerFactory`) all ship as dedicated factories built on the shared `UndyingFactory.Build` helper; Butcher Ghoul still auto-binds via `KeywordBinder` + `UndyingFactory` against any Scryfall row whose oracle prints the Undying keyword. Chord of Calling done. Eldritch Evolution done — tutor-with-sac is a primary engine starter for the deck. Green Sun's Zenith done — `GreenSunsZenithFactory` ({X}{G} Sorcery — tutor green creature mv ≤ X → battlefield with ZoneService-routed ETB triggers + printed self-shuffle into owner's library per CR 608.2c override, mirroring `EldritchEvolutionFactory`'s self-exile sequencing); the deck's premier toolbox tutor, fetches Wall of Roots / Dryad Arbor / Yawgmoth itself / Strangleroot Geist on curve. Eternal Witness done (ETB return-target-card-from-graveyard-to-hand; agent target + first-card fallback; "you may" auto-accepted v1; card-type agnostic) — recursion glue for the deck's Yawgmoth's Will / Chord / Eldritch Evolution loops. Wall of Roots done (Defender + once-per-turn place-(-0/-1)-counter-on-self mana ability via the new no-tap ManaAbility overload — green ramp into turn-3 Yawgmoth + counter-manipulation synergy with the -1/-1 sac engine). Ignoble Hierarch done (mono-G BRG mana-fixer + Exalted on a 0/1 Goblin Shaman body — same shape as Noble Hierarch with the mana colours Jund-aligned for the Yawgmoth shell). Soul-Scar Mage done — every noncombat damage event from a controller-owned source stamps -1/-1 counters on opponent creatures, feeding Yawgmoth's sac-a-Human-for-proliferate engine with a steady stream of -1/-1 fodder (Prowess on a 1-mana 1/2 body is the cheapest Yawgmoth-pillar -1/-1 feeder in the format). Malevolent Rumble done as a dedicated `MalevolentRumbleFactory` alongside the existing `MalevolentRumblePatternTemplate` (both bodies share `TokenFactory.CreateEldraziSpawn`) — the deck's MH3 selection + ramp engine: reveal-4 / take-the-first-permanent acts as a 4-deep tutor for Yawgmoth + Wall of Roots + Grist, the discarded 3 cards feed Dredger's Insight's lifegain trigger when they later leave the graveyard, and the 0/1 Eldrazi Spawn token is a one-shot {C} ritual + free sac fodder for Yawgmoth's pay-1-life-sac-a-creature ability (pairs particularly well with Bowmasters' amass + Grist's -2 self-sac to stretch a single Rumble into multiple Yawg activations). ~87%.
- **Domain Zoo** — Mid. Boros Charm done, fetches done, shocks done, Tribal Flames done, Scion of Draco's domain cost-reduction done (keyword-grant rider deferred), Territorial Kavu done (domain pump + attack loot). Ignoble Hierarch done (mono-G BRG mana-fixer + Exalted — the deck's signature MH3 turn-1 dork, replaces Noble Hierarch in the Jund-leaning colour pie). ~55%.
- **Amulet Titan** — Mid. Amulet of Vigor done (untap-on-enters-tapped trigger) + Primeval Titan done (ETB + attack land-tutor for up to 2, tapped) + Wall of Roots done (cost-counter green ramp — Defender wall that adds {G} once per turn without tapping). No bounce lands. ~35%.
- **Lurrus Companion** — Low-mid. Lurrus of the Dream-Den done (Lifelink + once-per-turn cast-permanent-mv≤2-from-graveyard; companion deck-construction rule deferred). Pairs with the existing low-mv permanent suite (Mishra's Bauble, Dryad Arbor, Stoneforge Mystic, Walking Ballista, Dark Confidant, etc.). Deck-construction enforcement absent. ~30%.
- **Hammer Time / Equipment** — Mid-high. Stoneforge Mystic done. Colossus Hammer done (+10/+0 + lose flying via AttachedBoostEffect + new LoseKeywordEffect). Sigarda's Aid done (flash-grant on Equipment/Aura via FlashGrantRegistry + ETB-attach rider). Puresteel Paladin done (Equipment-ETB draw trigger + zero-equip-cost lifecycle binder that gates on ≥3 artifacts; equip cost now wired through `EquipActivatedAbility.CostProvider` so the printed cost flips to {0} at pay time when controller has ≥3 artifacts). Sword of Fire and Ice done (markers-on-the-card path; DEBT-A grant deferred). Sword of Feast and Famine done (lifecycle-attached `ProtectionAbility("black")` + `ProtectionAbility("green")` via two `AttachedAuraAbilityGrantStaticEffect` so the equipped creature surfaces protection through `Rules.Protection.HasProtectionFromColor` — closes the grant-on-attach gap for this Sword; combat trigger has damaged player discard + untaps controller's lands). `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive (CR 702.6) now ships as a first-class `ActivatedAbility` subclass that encapsulates the sorcery-speed gate, target-creature-you-control candidate-gathering, attach-on-resolve, and a `CostProvider` delegate for Puresteel-style zero-equip — all 6 retrofitted equipment factories (Colossus Hammer, Skullclamp, Umezawa's Jitte, Sword of Fire and Ice, Sword of Feast and Famine, Cori-Steel Cutter) replace their hand-rolled `ActivatedAbility` + closure with one constructor call. Shadowspear done (+1/+1 + Trample + Lifelink via paired AttachedBoostEffect; `{1}, {T}` activated keyword-strip via new `LoseKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` — generic sibling of `LoseKeywordEffect` for "target creature loses X EOT" Layer 6 grants). Inquisitor's Flail done (Equipment {2} + Equip {2} + both printed damage-doubling replacements — source-side + target-side `DamageDoubleReplacement` registrations gated on `DamageIntent.IsCombatDamage` + `AttachedTo`; CR 510.1c discriminator added to `DamageIntent` so combat damage is distinguishable from spell / ping damage at the replacement layer). ~65%.
- **Merfolk** — Mid-high. Aether Vial done (mana-free creature-cheater that's the deck's engine). Spreading Seas done. Lord of Atlantis done (symmetric lord). Master of the Pearl Trident done (controller-only lord). Mystic Sanctuary done (ETB recur). Noble Hierarch done (Exalted + 3-color mana). Silvergill Adept done (ETB cantrip — reveal-or-pay enforcement deferred). Cursecatcher done (sac-counter activated ability). Merfolk Trickster done (Flash + ETB tap + lose-abilities EOT). ~85%.
- **Death and Taxes** — Low-mid. Aether Vial done (mana-free creature drop — the deck's signature). Stoneforge Mystic done. Solitude done. Thalia, Guardian of Thraben done (noncreature tax + First Strike). Skyclave Apparition + Leonin Arbiter absent. ~45%.
- **Boros Energy / Boros Convoke** — Mid. Guide of Souls done (1-drop {W} 1/2 anchor: ETB energy on every small-creature drop including itself + {E}{E} pump-and-flying = mana engine + finisher pumper). Aether Hub done (energy land + WUBRG fixer via Pay {E}). Amped Raptor done (ETB exile-4 cast-mv-≤-2-for-free body that doubles as cascade engine). Monastery Swiftspear done (Prowess 1-drop). Phlage done (recursion finisher). `Player.EnergyCounters` + `Player.GainEnergy` / `Player.PayEnergy` ledger + new `PayEnergyCost` ICost shape make the energy substrate first-class. Missing: Ocelot Pride (energy-conditional Cat tokens at upkeep — the other 1-drop), Static Discharge (energy-X burn), Voice of Victory (Convoke pump anchor). ~40%.

## Top 20 Modern staples NOT yet implemented

Sorted roughly by build priority (small infra lift × high meta share). Refreshed against origin/main `2182f0d` (post-Champion of the Parish + Thalia's Lieutenant). Round 5 of the refresh — round 4's list shipped wholesale (Anger of the Gods, Pyroclasm, Arclight Phoenix, Phoenix of Ash, Goblin Rabblemaster, Spellskite, plus Mystic Sanctuary, Engineered Plague, Search for Tomorrow, Territorial Kavu, Thalia, Noble Hierarch, Lord of Atlantis, Master of the Pearl Trident, Champion of the Parish, Thalia's Lieutenant, Remand, Mana Leak, Kitchen Finks).

Vintage / Legacy-only cards (Power Nine, original dual lands, Mind's Desire, Yawgmoth's Bargain, Library of Alexandria, Channel, Mox Pearl / Sapphire / Jet / Ruby / Emerald, Sneak Attack, Show and Tell, etc.) are deliberately excluded — this list tracks Modern-format staples only. (Dark Ritual, Sol Ring, Mana Vault, Lotus Petal, and Mana Crypt landed as named factories for Commander/historical-format coverage even though they're not Modern-legal.)

| # | Card | Difficulty | Blocker |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Cursecatcher | low | Creature — Merfolk Wizard {U} 1/1 (SHM) — {U}, Sacrifice Cursecatcher: counter target spell unless its controller pays {1}. Single activated ability (CR 602) with `ManaCostCost("{U}")` + `AdditionalCost.Sacrifice`; resolve logic mirrors Pact of Negation's "unless controller pays" gate (CR 118.4) via `PayMana({1})` no-op on success + `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` on failure. Sacrifice performed inline in the effect body (same shape as Goblin Welder / Goblin Engineer's self-sacrifice). "Sacrifice as additional cost" vs. "sacrifice as part of the activated ability cost" distinction: Cursecatcher's sacrifice is an activation cost, not an ETB, so the ability is on the stack before the creature leaves. Modern Merfolk backbone. |
| 2 | Silvergill Adept | low-medium | Creature — Merfolk Wizard {1}{U} 2/1 (LRW / MH2) — when Silvergill Adept enters, reveal a Merfolk card from your hand or pay {3}; if you revealed, draw a card. ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) with a branch: if the controller has a Merfolk card in hand, reveal it and draw 1; otherwise pay {3} (static prompt deferred — v1 auto-reveals first Merfolk in hand, falls back to paying if none). ETB draw via `Player.DrawCard`. Reveal-or-pay conditional shaped like Selfless Spirit / Waterfront Bouncer's cost branch — no existing reveal-from-hand primitive yet (gap). Modern Merfolk card advantage engine. |
| 3 | Mantis Rider | low | Creature — Human Monk {W}{U}{R} 3/3 Flying + Vigilance + Haste (KTK). Three keyword markers on a vanilla 3/3 body — no printed triggers or activated abilities. Tri-color casting cost is the main complexity (all three coloured pips; `ManaCost.Parse` handles it cleanly). Closes the Humans / Jeskai archetype gap. Modern Humans 4-drop. |
| 4 | Reflector Mage | medium | Creature — Human Wizard {1}{W}{U} 2/3 (OGW) — ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) with a 1..1 "target creature you don't own" `TargetRequest` (Intent: `BotIntent.Removal`): on resolution bounces the chosen creature to its owner's hand (zone-move Battlefield→Hand via ZoneService, CR 701.20) and stamps a "name restriction" preventing its controller from casting a spell with that name until your next turn. Name-restriction needs a new `CastingRestrictions.AddNameBan(player, cardName, untilEndOfControllerTurn)` slot + `ActionValidator.ValidateCastSpell` consult (CR 601.2a — no existing per-name cast ban surface). Modern Humans / Bant pillar. |
| 5 | Containment Priest | medium | Creature — Human Cleric {1}{W} 2/2 Flash (M21 / MH2) — printed static "if a nontoken permanent would enter the battlefield and it wasn't cast, exile it instead." Needs a global enters-battlefield replacement effect (CR 614.1c) registered on the `ReplacementBus` while Containment Priest is on the battlefield, keyed on `ZoneMoveIntent` to-Battlefield with `!WasCast` (the cast-marker gap on `Card` is the primary blocker — same gap noted on The One Ring's ETB protection clause; `SpellCastFlow` stamps `WasCast=true` on the stack object but the flag is not yet threaded into the resulting permanent on battlefield entry). Modern Death and Taxes / Hatebears sideboard. |
| 6 | Meddling Mage | medium | Creature — Human Wizard {W}{U} 2/2 (INV / MH2) — ETB "name a nonland card"; printed static prevents its controller's opponents from casting spells with that name. Two surfaces: (1) ETB agent prompt to name a card — uses the same `NameACardAction` stub as `PithingNeedleFactory` (name stored on the card instance); (2) `CastingRestrictions.AddNameBan(opponent, namedCard)` static — same name-ban slot needed by Reflector Mage (#4). Both blockers resolved together. Modern Humans / Taxes sideboard. |
| 7 | Skyclave Apparition | medium | Creature — Kor Spirit {1}{W}{W} 2/2 (ZNR) — ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a): exile target nonland, nontoken permanent an opponent controls with mana value ≤ 4. LTB triggered ability (CR 603.6a): when Skyclave Apparition leaves the battlefield, the exiled permanent's owner creates a token that's a copy of it (unless it was exiled by Skyclave's own ETB and is still in exile). ETB exile into a private exile zone + LTB "create token copy of the exiled card" pairs the exile-host pattern (see Spell Queller's exile→LTB return) with a token-copy variant. Token copy needs `TokenFactory.CreateCopyOf(card)` (CopyEffect-shape). MV ≤ 4 gate enforced at resolve (CR 608.2b). Modern Death and Taxes / Taxes pillar. |
| 8 | Leonin Arbiter | low-medium | Creature — Cat Cleric {1}{W} 2/2 (SOM) — printed static "players can't search libraries unless they pay {2}." Needs a `LibrarySearchRestrictionEffect` (CR 701.19 — any library search requires paying {2}; unpaid → may not search) registered on a `LibrarySearchRestrictions` slot similar to `CastingRestrictions`; `SearchSpellFactory.SearchLibrary` and tutor resolve paths consult it. Payment prompt deferred (same queue as Pact cycle). Modern Death and Taxes pillar. |
| 9 | Witch Enchanter // Witch-Enchanter's Wishboard | high | Creature — Human Druid {2}{W} 2/3 (MH3) DFC — front face flashes in to counter target activated ability; transform on cast. Back face is the Wishboard land with the static "you may look at and cast cards from outside the game you own" (Karn the Great Creator's wishboard slot). Front is a v1-easy activated-ability counter (CR 701.5 — counter only activateds, not triggereds); transform-on-cast trigger uses the `MdfcState` flipper from `AjaniNacatlPariahFactory`. Modern Cascade / Tron sideboard. |
| 12 | Temur Battle Rage | low | Instant {R} (GTC) — +1/+0 + double strike + trample until EOT to target creature you control; ferocious rider deferred (no ferocious surface yet). Reuses Goblin Bushwhacker-shape EOT pump + Layer-6 keyword grant. Modern Death's Shadow / Infect finisher. |
| 13 | Simian Spirit Guide | low | Creature — Ape Spirit {2}{R} 2/2 (PLC) — exile from hand: add {R}. Spell-cost alt that exiles from-hand to make 1 red mana. Reuses Elvish Spirit Guide's exile-from-hand mana shape (Elvish Spirit Guide is already shipped — copy it). Modern Cascade / Goblins ramp. |
| 14 | Boseiju, Who Endures | medium | Legendary Land — Channel ability {1}{G}, Discard: destroy target nonbasic land, artifact, or enchantment; the land's controller may search for a basic land. Channel (CR 702.74) is a discard-cost activated ability that targets while the card is in hand; needs an activated-ability-from-hand surface or a Cycling-like shortcut. Modern Tron / Amulet hate piece. **Note: a `boseiju.json` JSON card already exists — verify before re-shipping.** |
| 15 | Simic Growth Chamber + Gruul Turf + other bounce lands | medium | Lands — ETB return a land you control to its owner's hand; tap for two of two colours. ETB self-bounce-required-cost + dual mana ability. Unlocks Amulet Titan archetype to ~60%. |
| 16 | Indomitable Creativity | medium | Sorcery {X}{R}{R} (AKH) — destroy X target artifacts and/or creatures; for each one, reveal cards from top until a non-land permanent card. Multi-target destroy + reveal-until-permanent loop. Modern 4c Creativity pillar. |

## How to update this doc

After merging a card-shipping PR:
1. Append the new card to the **Named factories** table (or **Template-covered (notable)** if it's a template port).
2. Remove it from **Top 20 not yet implemented** if it appears there.
3. Run `./scripts/update-coverage-headline.sh` to refresh the **Headline numbers** table from disk.
4. Bump the **Last updated** date at the top.

After merging a mechanic-infra PR:
1. Flip the relevant row in **Costs**, **Effects**, **Keywords**, or **Targeting / cast flow** to **Done (#PR)**.
2. If a top-20 entry's blocker is resolved, drop its difficulty one tier in the priority table.

### Multi-`[CardName]` for functional reprints

When two cards share an **identical resolve body** (e.g. Wrath of God +
Damnation — same sweep, different printed cost/colour), don't write two
factories. Stack multiple `[CardName]` attributes on the canonical factory
and expose a `public static <Card> Create(Player owner, string cardName)`
overload that branches on the requested name to produce the right printed
name + cost. The source generator
(`Majik.Core.SourceGen.NamedCardFactoryGenerator`) detects the second-arg
overload and dispatches each `[CardName]` through `Create(owner, name)`;
the plain `Create(owner)` overload stays as a convenience for callers
that don't care about the reprint variant. Headline factory-count drops
by one per collapsed wrapper — that's expected and the script handles it.

Don't apply this to **parametric cycles** (fetchlands, pact cycle, Mox
cycle) — those have per-card numeric or per-card-flavour differences and
stay as their own factories.

CI/pre-commit can call `./scripts/update-coverage-headline.sh --check` to fail when the headline counts drift from on-disk reality.

Keep this file terse. New cards = one row. New mechanics = one row. Long-form rationale lives in PR descriptions, not here.
